    <script type="text/javascript">
window.sendCtaData = function(actionType, actionTarget, userKey) {
  if (typeof axios === 'undefined') {
    return Promise.resolve();
  }

  return axios.post('/api/guest/cta/store', {
    user: userKey || window.ctaUserKey || window.location.hostname,
    action_type: actionType,
    action_target: actionTarget || window.location.href
  }, { skipMitraLoading: true }).catch(function(error) {
    console.log(error);
  });
};
</script>
    <script type="text/javascript">
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        if (typeof sendCtaData === 'function') {
          sendCtaData('guest_customer_login_page', window.location.href);
        }
      });
    </script>

    <script type="text/javascript">
        const global_mail = null;
        const global_pass = "";

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            const vue = new Vue({
                el: '#app',
                data: {
                    email2: '',
                    loading: false,
                    seconds: 0,
                    email: global_mail || '',
                    password: global_pass || '',
                    passwordVisible: false,
                    login_loading: false,
                    showForgot: false,
                    forgotFeedback: null
                },
                mounted() {
                    if (localStorage["countdownForgotPasswordCustomer"]) {
                        this.seconds = Number(localStorage["countdownForgotPasswordCustomer"]) || 0;
                        if (this.seconds > 0) {
                            this.runCountdown();
                        }
                    }
                },
                methods: {
                    setForgotFeedback(type, message) {
                        this.forgotFeedback = { type: type, message: message };
                    },
                    sendEmail() {
                        if (this.loading || this.seconds > 0) {
                            return;
                        }

                        const email = (this.email2 || '').trim();
                        if (!email) {
                            this.setForgotFeedback('error', 'Email tidak boleh kosong');
                            return;
                        }

                        this.loading = true;
                        this.forgotFeedback = null;

                        const formData = new FormData();
                        formData.append('email', email);

                        axios.post('/api/guest/forgot-password', formData)
                            .then(r => {
                                const message = (r && r.data && r.data.message)
                                    ? r.data.message
                                    : 'Jika email terdaftar, link reset password telah dikirim.';
                                this.setForgotFeedback('success', message);
                                this.seconds = 60;
                                this.runCountdown();
                            })
                            .catch(err => {
                                const message = (err && err.response && err.response.data && err.response.data.message)
                                    ? err.response.data.message
                                    : 'Gagal mengirim link reset password. Coba lagi sebentar.';
                                this.setForgotFeedback('error', message);
                            })
                            .finally(() => {
                                this.loading = false;
                            });
                    },
                    runCountdown() {
                        const makeIteration = () => {
                            if (this.seconds > 0) {
                                this.seconds -= 1;
                                localStorage['countdownForgotPasswordCustomer'] = this.seconds;
                                setTimeout(makeIteration, 1000);
                            } else {
                                localStorage.removeItem('countdownForgotPasswordCustomer');
                            }
                        };
                        setTimeout(makeIteration, 1000);
                    }
                }
            });
        });
    </script>

<script>
(function () {
    var GAP = 12;
    var nav = document.getElementById('rg-nav');
    var spacer = document.getElementById('rg-nav-spacer');
    var btn = document.getElementById('rg-mobile-menu-btn');
    var menu = document.getElementById('rg-mobile-menu');
    var icon = document.getElementById('rg-mobile-menu-icon');

    function syncOffset() {
        if (!nav) return;
        var offset = Math.ceil(nav.getBoundingClientRect().bottom) + GAP;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--rg-nav-offset', offset + 'px');
        if (spacer) spacer.style.height = offset + 'px';
    }

    function onScroll() {
        if (!nav) return;
        nav.classList.toggle('rg-nav-scrolled', window.scrollY > 12);
    }

    function normalizePath(pathname) {
        var p = (pathname || '/').replace(/\/+$/, '');
        return p === '' ? '/' : p;
    }

    function syncActiveNav() {
        var path = normalizePath(window.location.pathname);
        var hash = (window.location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
        var links = document.querySelectorAll('[data-rg-nav]');
        var activeKey = '';

        if (path.indexOf('about-us') !== -1) {
            activeKey = hash === 'pricing' ? 'pricing' : 'feature';
        } else if (path.indexOf('product') !== -1) {
            activeKey = 'product';
        } else if (path.indexOf('blog') !== -1) {
            activeKey = 'blog';
        } else if (path === '/') {
            activeKey = hash === 'katalog-tema' ? 'tema' : 'home';
        }

        links.forEach(function (link) {
            var key = link.getAttribute('data-rg-nav');
            link.classList.toggle('is-active', key === activeKey);
        });
    }

    function setMenuOpen(open) {
        if (!menu || !btn) return;
        menu.classList.toggle('hidden', !open);
        if (nav) nav.classList.toggle('rg-nav-open', open);
        btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');
        if (icon) {
            icon.classList.toggle('fa-bars', !open);
            icon.classList.toggle('fa-times', open);
        }
        document.body.style.overflow = open ? 'hidden' : '';
        syncOffset();
    }

    syncOffset();
    onScroll();
    syncActiveNav();
    window.addEventListener('load', syncOffset);
    window.addEventListener('resize', syncOffset);
    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', syncActiveNav);

    if (typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined' && nav) {
        new ResizeObserver(syncOffset).observe(nav);
    }

    if (btn && menu) {
        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
            setMenuOpen(menu.classList.contains('hidden'));
        });
        menu.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(function (link) {
            link.addEventListener('click', function () { setMenuOpen(false); });
        });
    }
})();
</script>

                <div class="w-full">
                    <div class="rg-card-elevate rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl bg-white shadow-lg md:shadow-xl border border-rosegold-primary/5 text-center overflow-hidden flex flex-col h-full">
                        <div class="p-3 sm:p-4 md:p-6 flex flex-col flex-1">

<script>
(function() {
    function fallbackBtn() {
        var nodes = document.querySelectorAll('#modern-wa-btn');
        for (var i = 1; i < nodes.length; i++) nodes[i].remove();
        return nodes[0] || null;
    }

    function hideIfCustomWa() {
        var btn = fallbackBtn();
        if (!btn) return false;
        // Only hide fallback when floating widget button is actually rendered
        var customBtn = document.querySelector('.floating-wpp .floating-wpp-button');
        if (customBtn && customBtn.offsetWidth > 0 && customBtn.offsetHeight > 0) {
            btn.style.display = 'none';
            return true;
        }
        btn.style.display = '';
        return false;
    }

    function bindDrag(btn) {
        if (!btn || btn.dataset.waBound) return;
        btn.dataset.waBound = '1';
        let offset = { x: 0, y: 0 };
        let isDown = false;

        btn.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {
        isDown = true;
        offset = {
            x: btn.offsetLeft - e.clientX,
            y: btn.offsetTop - e.clientY
        };
        btn.style.transition = "none";
        document.body.style.userSelect = "none";
    }, false);

    document.addEventListener('mouseup', function() {
        isDown = false;
        btn.style.transition = "";
        document.body.style.userSelect = "";
    }, false);

    document.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e) {
        if (!isDown) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        let newLeft = e.clientX + offset.x;
        let newTop = e.clientY + offset.y;
        newLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerWidth - btn.offsetWidth, newLeft));
        newTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight - btn.offsetHeight, newTop));
        btn.style.left = newLeft + "px";
        btn.style.top = newTop + "px";
        btn.style.right = "auto";
        btn.style.bottom = "auto";
    }, false);

    btn.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) {
        isDown = true;
        const touch = e.touches[0];
        offset = {
            x: btn.offsetLeft - touch.clientX,
            y: btn.offsetTop - touch.clientY
        };
        btn.style.transition = "none";
    }, false);

    btn.addEventListener('touchend', function() {
        isDown = false;
        btn.style.transition = "";
    }, false);

    btn.addEventListener('touchmove', function(e) {
        if (!isDown) return;
        const touch = e.touches[0];
        let newLeft = touch.clientX + offset.x;
        let newTop = touch.clientY + offset.y;
        newLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerWidth - btn.offsetWidth, newLeft));
        newTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight - btn.offsetHeight, newTop));
        btn.style.left = newLeft + "px";
        btn.style.top = newTop + "px";
        btn.style.right = "auto";
        btn.style.bottom = "auto";
    }, { passive: true });

        function setInitialPosition() {
            btn.style.left = "";
            btn.style.top = "";
            btn.style.right = "1rem";
            btn.style.bottom = "1.25rem";
        }
        setInitialPosition();
        window.addEventListener('resize', setInitialPosition);
    }

    function sync() {
        var btn = fallbackBtn();
        if (btn) bindDrag(btn);
        hideIfCustomWa();
    }

    document.addEventListener('mitra:ready', sync);
    sync();
    setTimeout(sync, 700);
})();
</script>
<script>
(function () {
    var reduceMotion = window.matchMedia ? window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)') : null;
    var selector = [
        '[class*=\"animate-\"]',
        '.blob',
        '.fade-in',
        '.still-track',
        '.cover-slide-img',
        '.pc-hero__glow-orb'
    ].join(',');
    var nodes = [];
    var observer = null;

    function setPaused(el, paused) {
        if (!el || !el.style) return;
        el.style.animationPlayState = paused ? 'paused' : 'running';
    }

    function pauseByVisibility() {
        var hidden = document.hidden || (reduceMotion && reduceMotion.matches);
        for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
            if (hidden) {
                setPaused(nodes[i], true);
            } else if (!observer) {
                setPaused(nodes[i], false);
            } else {
                setPaused(nodes[i], nodes[i].__ajOffscreen === true);
            }
        }
    }

    function bindObserver() {
        if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) return;
        observer = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
            entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                entry.target.__ajOffscreen = !entry.isIntersecting;
                setPaused(entry.target, document.hidden || entry.target.__ajOffscreen || (reduceMotion && reduceMotion.matches));
            });
        }, { threshold: 0.05 });
        for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) observer.observe(nodes[i]);
    }

    function refreshMotionNodes() {
        if (observer) observer.disconnect();
        observer = null;
        nodes = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(selector));
        if (!nodes.length) return;
        if (!(reduceMotion && reduceMotion.matches)) bindObserver();
        pauseByVisibility();
    }

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', pauseByVisibility);
    document.addEventListener('mitra:ready', refreshMotionNodes);
    if (reduceMotion && typeof reduceMotion.addEventListener === 'function') {
        reduceMotion.addEventListener('change', refreshMotionNodes);
    }
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', refreshMotionNodes, { once: true });
    } else {
        refreshMotionNodes();
    }
})();
</script>

        function mountAboutPricingTabs() {
            var root = document.getElementById('pricing');
            if (!root || typeof window.Vue !== 'function' || root.__pricingVue__) return;

            var initialTab = 'website';
            if (!root.querySelector('#pills-website-tab')) {
                if (root.querySelector('#pills-savethedate-tab')) initialTab = 'savethedate';
                else if (root.querySelector('#pills-otherEvents-tab')) initialTab = 'otherEvents';
            }

            root.__pricingVue__ = new Vue({
                el: '#pricing',
                data: {
                    activePricingTab: initialTab
                }
            });
        }

            });

        });

<script>
(function () {
    var GAP = 12;
    var nav = document.getElementById('rg-nav');
    var spacer = document.getElementById('rg-nav-spacer');
    var btn = document.getElementById('rg-mobile-menu-btn');
    var menu = document.getElementById('rg-mobile-menu');
    var icon = document.getElementById('rg-mobile-menu-icon');

    function syncOffset() {
        if (!nav) return;
        var offset = Math.ceil(nav.getBoundingClientRect().bottom) + GAP;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--rg-nav-offset', offset + 'px');
        if (spacer) spacer.style.height = offset + 'px';
    }

    function onScroll() {
        if (!nav) return;
        nav.classList.toggle('rg-nav-scrolled', window.scrollY > 12);
    }

    function normalizePath(pathname) {
        var p = (pathname || '/').replace(/\/+$/, '');
        return p === '' ? '/' : p;
    }

    function syncActiveNav() {
        var path = normalizePath(window.location.pathname);
        var hash = (window.location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
        var links = document.querySelectorAll('[data-rg-nav]');
        var activeKey = '';

        if (path.indexOf('about-us') !== -1) {
            activeKey = hash === 'pricing' ? 'pricing' : 'feature';
        } else if (path.indexOf('product') !== -1) {
            activeKey = 'product';
        } else if (path.indexOf('blog') !== -1) {
            activeKey = 'blog';
        } else if (path === '/') {
            activeKey = hash === 'katalog-tema' ? 'tema' : 'home';
        }

        links.forEach(function (link) {
            var key = link.getAttribute('data-rg-nav');
            link.classList.toggle('is-active', key === activeKey);
        });
    }

    function setMenuOpen(open) {
        if (!menu || !btn) return;
        menu.classList.toggle('hidden', !open);
        if (nav) nav.classList.toggle('rg-nav-open', open);
        btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');
        if (icon) {
            icon.classList.toggle('fa-bars', !open);
            icon.classList.toggle('fa-times', open);
        }
        document.body.style.overflow = open ? 'hidden' : '';
        syncOffset();
    }

    syncOffset();
    onScroll();
    syncActiveNav();
    window.addEventListener('load', syncOffset);
    window.addEventListener('resize', syncOffset);
    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', syncActiveNav);

    if (typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined' && nav) {
        new ResizeObserver(syncOffset).observe(nav);
    }

    if (btn && menu) {
        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
            setMenuOpen(menu.classList.contains('hidden'));
        });
        menu.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(function (link) {
            link.addEventListener('click', function () { setMenuOpen(false); });
        });
    }
})();
</script>

<script>
(function() {
    function fallbackBtn() {
        var nodes = document.querySelectorAll('#modern-wa-btn');
        for (var i = 1; i < nodes.length; i++) nodes[i].remove();
        return nodes[0] || null;
    }

    function hideIfCustomWa() {
        var btn = fallbackBtn();
        if (!btn) return false;
        // Only hide fallback when floating widget button is actually rendered
        var customBtn = document.querySelector('.floating-wpp .floating-wpp-button');
        if (customBtn && customBtn.offsetWidth > 0 && customBtn.offsetHeight > 0) {
            btn.style.display = 'none';
            return true;
        }
        btn.style.display = '';
        return false;
    }

    function bindDrag(btn) {
        if (!btn || btn.dataset.waBound) return;
        btn.dataset.waBound = '1';
        let offset = { x: 0, y: 0 };
        let isDown = false;

        btn.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {
        isDown = true;
        offset = {
            x: btn.offsetLeft - e.clientX,
            y: btn.offsetTop - e.clientY
        };
        btn.style.transition = "none";
        document.body.style.userSelect = "none";
    }, false);

    document.addEventListener('mouseup', function() {
        isDown = false;
        btn.style.transition = "";
        document.body.style.userSelect = "";
    }, false);

    document.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e) {
        if (!isDown) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        let newLeft = e.clientX + offset.x;
        let newTop = e.clientY + offset.y;
        newLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerWidth - btn.offsetWidth, newLeft));
        newTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight - btn.offsetHeight, newTop));
        btn.style.left = newLeft + "px";
        btn.style.top = newTop + "px";
        btn.style.right = "auto";
        btn.style.bottom = "auto";
    }, false);

    btn.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) {
        isDown = true;
        const touch = e.touches[0];
        offset = {
            x: btn.offsetLeft - touch.clientX,
            y: btn.offsetTop - touch.clientY
        };
        btn.style.transition = "none";
    }, false);

    btn.addEventListener('touchend', function() {
        isDown = false;
        btn.style.transition = "";
    }, false);

    btn.addEventListener('touchmove', function(e) {
        if (!isDown) return;
        const touch = e.touches[0];
        let newLeft = touch.clientX + offset.x;
        let newTop = touch.clientY + offset.y;
        newLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerWidth - btn.offsetWidth, newLeft));
        newTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight - btn.offsetHeight, newTop));
        btn.style.left = newLeft + "px";
        btn.style.top = newTop + "px";
        btn.style.right = "auto";
        btn.style.bottom = "auto";
    }, { passive: true });

        function setInitialPosition() {
            btn.style.left = "";
            btn.style.top = "";
            btn.style.right = "1rem";
            btn.style.bottom = "1.25rem";
        }
        setInitialPosition();
        window.addEventListener('resize', setInitialPosition);
    }

    function sync() {
        var btn = fallbackBtn();
        if (btn) bindDrag(btn);
        hideIfCustomWa();
    }

    document.addEventListener('mitra:ready', sync);
    sync();
    setTimeout(sync, 700);
})();
</script>
<script>
(function () {
    var reduceMotion = window.matchMedia ? window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)') : null;
    var selector = [
        '[class*=\"animate-\"]',
        '.blob',
        '.fade-in',
        '.still-track',
        '.cover-slide-img',
        '.pc-hero__glow-orb'
    ].join(',');
    var nodes = [];
    var observer = null;

    function setPaused(el, paused) {
        if (!el || !el.style) return;
        el.style.animationPlayState = paused ? 'paused' : 'running';
    }

    function pauseByVisibility() {
        var hidden = document.hidden || (reduceMotion && reduceMotion.matches);
        for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
            if (hidden) {
                setPaused(nodes[i], true);
            } else if (!observer) {
                setPaused(nodes[i], false);
            } else {
                setPaused(nodes[i], nodes[i].__ajOffscreen === true);
            }
        }
    }

    function bindObserver() {
        if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) return;
        observer = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
            entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                entry.target.__ajOffscreen = !entry.isIntersecting;
                setPaused(entry.target, document.hidden || entry.target.__ajOffscreen || (reduceMotion && reduceMotion.matches));
            });
        }, { threshold: 0.05 });
        for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) observer.observe(nodes[i]);
    }

    function refreshMotionNodes() {
        if (observer) observer.disconnect();
        observer = null;
        nodes = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(selector));
        if (!nodes.length) return;
        if (!(reduceMotion && reduceMotion.matches)) bindObserver();
        pauseByVisibility();
    }

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', pauseByVisibility);
    document.addEventListener('mitra:ready', refreshMotionNodes);
    if (reduceMotion && typeof reduceMotion.addEventListener === 'function') {
        reduceMotion.addEventListener('change', refreshMotionNodes);
    }
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', refreshMotionNodes, { once: true });
    } else {
        refreshMotionNodes();
    }
})();
</script>

    <script src="/admin-theme/dist/js/axios.min.js"></script>
    <script src="/admin-theme/dist/js/vue.min.js"></script>
    <script>
(function (root) {
    var HEX = /^#(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{3}|[0-9a-fA-F]{6})$/;
    var FAMILY_ORDER = ['gold', 'cream', 'maroon', 'black', 'green', 'blue', 'pink', 'white', 'brown', 'purple', 'gray'];
    var FAMILY_LABEL = {
        gold: 'Emas',
        cream: 'Cream',
        maroon: 'Maroon',
        black: 'Hitam',
        green: 'Hijau',
        blue: 'Biru',
        pink: 'Pink',
        white: 'Putih',
        brown: 'Cokelat',
        purple: 'Ungu',
        gray: 'Abu'
    };
    var FAMILY_SWATCH = {
        gold: '#C5A059',
        cream: '#F7F2EA',
        maroon: '#6B2D3C',
        black: '#1C0F12',
        green: '#3D8B5A',
        blue: '#336D9A',
        pink: '#E07A9A',
        white: '#FFFFFF',
        brown: '#5D3317',
        purple: '#8B5FBF',
        gray: '#8E8E8E'
    };

    function normalizeHex(value) {
        if (typeof value !== 'string') return null;
        var hex = value.trim();
        if (!HEX.test(hex)) return null;
        if (hex.length === 4) {
            return ('#' + hex[1] + hex[1] + hex[2] + hex[2] + hex[3] + hex[3]).toUpperCase();
        }
        return hex.toUpperCase();
    }

    function colors(theme) {
        if (!theme) return [];
        var picked = [];
        var i;
        var hex;
        if (Object.prototype.toString.call(theme.colors) === '[object Array]') {
            for (i = 0; i < theme.colors.length; i++) {
                hex = normalizeHex(theme.colors[i]);
                if (hex) picked.push(hex);
            }
        }
        if (picked.length < 3) {
            var stored = [theme.color_1, theme.color_2, theme.color_3];
            picked = [];
            for (i = 0; i < stored.length; i++) {
                hex = normalizeHex(stored[i]);
                if (hex) picked.push(hex);
            }
        }
        return picked.length >= 3 ? picked.slice(0, 3) : [];
    }

    function hexToHsl(hex) {
        var n = normalizeHex(hex);
        if (!n) return null;
        var r = parseInt(n.slice(1, 3), 16) / 255;
        var g = parseInt(n.slice(3, 5), 16) / 255;
        var b = parseInt(n.slice(5, 7), 16) / 255;
        var max = Math.max(r, g, b);
        var min = Math.min(r, g, b);
        var l = (max + min) / 2;
        if (max === min) return { h: 0, s: 0, l: l };
        var d = max - min;
        var s = l > 0.5 ? d / (2 - max - min) : d / (max + min);
        var h = 0;
        if (max === r) h = ((g - b) / d + (g < b ? 6 : 0)) / 6;
        else if (max === g) h = ((b - r) / d + 2) / 6;
        else h = ((r - g) / d + 4) / 6;
        return { h: h * 360, s: s, l: l };
    }

    function colorFamily(hex) {
        var hsl = hexToHsl(hex);
        if (!hsl) return 'gray';
        var h = hsl.h;
        var s = hsl.s;
        var l = hsl.l;
        if (l <= 0.12) return 'black';
        if (l >= 0.93 && s < 0.12) return 'white';
        if (s < 0.1) return l >= 0.72 ? 'cream' : (l <= 0.28 ? 'black' : 'gray');
        if (l >= 0.82 && s < 0.55 && h >= 20 && h <= 70) return 'cream';
        if (h >= 28 && h <= 58 && s >= 0.22 && l >= 0.28 && l <= 0.78) return 'gold';
        if ((h <= 18 || h >= 345) && l < 0.4 && s >= 0.18) return 'maroon';
        if ((h <= 22 || h >= 318) && l >= 0.42) return 'pink';
        if (h >= 12 && h <= 46 && l < 0.48) return 'brown';
        if (h >= 70 && h <= 175) return 'green';
        if (h >= 185 && h <= 255) return 'blue';
        if (h >= 255 && h <= 320) return 'purple';
        if ((h <= 18 || h >= 345) && s >= 0.35) return 'maroon';
        return 'gray';
    }

    function themeFamilies(theme) {
        var set = {};
        var pal = colors(theme);
        var i;
        for (i = 0; i < pal.length; i++) {
            set[colorFamily(pal[i])] = true;
        }
        return FAMILY_ORDER.filter(function (family) { return set[family]; });
    }

    function asList(list) {
        if (!list) return [];
        if (typeof list === 'string') return [];
        if (Object.prototype.toString.call(list) === '[object Array]') return list;
        if (typeof list.length === 'number') {
            var out = [];
            var i;
            for (i = 0; i < list.length; i++) out.push(list[i]);
            return out;
        }
        return [];
    }

    function availableFamilies(list) {
        var present = {};
        var themes = asList(list);
        var i;
        var j;
        var fams;
        for (i = 0; i < themes.length; i++) {
            fams = themeFamilies(themes[i]);
            for (j = 0; j < fams.length; j++) present[fams[j]] = true;
        }
        return FAMILY_ORDER.filter(function (family) { return present[family]; });
    }

    function matches(theme, selected) {
        var chosen = asList(selected);
        if (!chosen.length) return true;
        var fams = themeFamilies(theme);
        var i;
        for (i = 0; i < chosen.length; i++) {
            if (fams.indexOf(chosen[i]) !== -1) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    function filterList(list, selected) {
        var themes = asList(list);
        if (!asList(selected).length) return themes;
        return themes.filter(function (theme) { return matches(theme, selected); });
    }

    function themeTitle(theme) {
        if (!theme) return '';
        var name = theme.name || theme.title || '';
        return String(name).toLowerCase();
    }

    function matchesQuery(theme, query) {
        var q = String(query || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!q) return true;
        return themeTitle(theme).indexOf(q) !== -1;
    }

    root.AjakanThemePalette = {
        mixin: {
            data: function () {
                return {
                    selectedColorFamilies: [],
                    themeSearchQuery: '',
                    isColorMenuOpen: false,
                    selectedType: null,
                    selectedSubTheme: null,
                    selectedSub: null,
                    loading: false,
                    page: 1,
                    perPage: 12,
                };
            },
            watch: {
                selectedType: function () {
                    this.selectedColorFamilies = [];
                    this.isColorMenuOpen = false;
                    this.page = 1;
                },
                selectedSubTheme: function () {
                    this.selectedColorFamilies = [];
                    this.isColorMenuOpen = false;
                    this.page = 1;
                },
                selectedSub: function () {
                    this.selectedColorFamilies = [];
                    this.isColorMenuOpen = false;
                    this.page = 1;
                },
                selectedColorFamilies: function () {
                    this.page = 1;
                },
                themeSearchQuery: function () {
                    this.page = 1;
                }
            },
            computed: {
                subThemeNameById: function () {
                    var mapping = {};
                    var append = function (list) {
                        asList(list).forEach(function (sub) {
                            if (!sub || typeof sub.id === 'undefined') return;
                            mapping[sub.id] = sub.name || '';
                        });
                    };
                    append(this.subTheme);
                    append(this.templates2 && this.templates2.sub_themes);
                    return mapping;
                },
                catalogItems: function () {
                    var source = [];
                    if (asList(this.theme).length) source = asList(this.theme);
                    else if (asList(this.listThemes).length) source = asList(this.listThemes);
                    else if (this.templates2 && asList(this.templates2.themes).length) source = asList(this.templates2.themes);
                    return this.paletteFilter(source);
                },
                pagerTotal: function () {
                    var perPage = Number(this.perPage || 12);
                    if (perPage < 1) perPage = 1;
                    var total = Math.ceil(this.catalogItems.length / perPage);
                    return total > 0 ? total : 1;
                },
                pagerPages: function () {
                    var total = this.pagerTotal;
                    var current = Number(this.page || 1);
                    if (current < 1) current = 1;
                    if (current > total) current = total;
                    var radius = 2;
                    var start = Math.max(1, current - radius);
                    var end = Math.min(total, current + radius);
                    var pages = [];
                    var p;
                    for (p = start; p <= end; p++) pages.push(p);
                    return pages;
                }
            },
            mounted: function () {
                var self = this;
                this._mtpDocClick = function (event) {
                    if (!self.isColorMenuOpen) return;
                    var el = event.target;
                    if (el && el.closest && el.closest('.mtp-color-root, .mtp-color-panel')) return;
                    self.isColorMenuOpen = false;
                };
                this._mtpDocKey = function (event) {
                    if (event.key === 'Escape') self.isColorMenuOpen = false;
                };
                document.addEventListener('click', this._mtpDocClick);
                document.addEventListener('keydown', this._mtpDocKey);
            },
            beforeDestroy: function () {
                if (this._mtpDocClick) document.removeEventListener('click', this._mtpDocClick);
                if (this._mtpDocKey) document.removeEventListener('keydown', this._mtpDocKey);
            },
            methods: {
                themePalette: colors,
                paletteFilter: function (list) {
                    var themes = filterList(list, this.selectedColorFamilies);
                    var query = this.themeSearchQuery;
                    if (!String(query || '').trim()) return themes;
                    return themes.filter(function (theme) { return matchesQuery(theme, query); });
                },
                pagedThemes: function (list) {
                    var filtered = this.paletteFilter(list);
                    var perPage = Number(this.perPage || 12);
                    if (perPage < 1) perPage = 1;
                    var total = Math.ceil(filtered.length / perPage);
                    if (!total) {
                        if (this.page !== 1) this.page = 1;
                        return [];
                    }
                    if (this.page > total) this.page = total;
                    if (this.page < 1) this.page = 1;
                    var start = (this.page - 1) * perPage;
                    return filtered.slice(start, start + perPage);
                },
                goPagerPage: function (nextPage) {
                    var target = Number(nextPage || 1);
                    if (target < 1) target = 1;
                    if (target > this.pagerTotal) target = this.pagerTotal;
                    this.page = target;
                },
                toggleColorMenu: function () {
                    this.isColorMenuOpen = !this.isColorMenuOpen;
                },
                colorFamilyOptions: function (list) {
                    return availableFamilies(list);
                },
                colorFamilyLabel: function (family) {
                    return FAMILY_LABEL[family] || family;
                },
                colorFamilySwatch: function (family) {
                    return FAMILY_SWATCH[family] || '#8E8E8E';
                },
                toggleColorFamily: function (family) {
                    var next = this.selectedColorFamilies.slice();
                    var idx = next.indexOf(family);
                    if (idx === -1) next.push(family);
                    else next.splice(idx, 1);
                    this.selectedColorFamilies = next;
                },
                clearColorFamilies: function () {
                    this.selectedColorFamilies = [];
                },
                fetchCatalogSubThemes: function (category) {
                    var type = category || this.selectedType;
                    var source = root.AjakanCatalog;
                    if (source && typeof source.fetchSubThemes === 'function') {
                        return source.fetchSubThemes(type);
                    }
                    if (!root.axios || typeof root.axios.get !== 'function') {
                        return Promise.resolve([]);
                    }
                    return root.axios.get('/api/guest/mitra/fetch-sub-themes', {
                        params: { category: type }
                    }).then(function (response) {
                        var payload = response && response.data && response.data.data;
                        return asList(payload);
                    });
                },
                fetchCatalogThemes: function (category, subThemeId) {
                    var type = category || this.selectedType;
                    var subTheme = typeof subThemeId === 'undefined' ? this.selectedSubTheme : subThemeId;
                    var source = root.AjakanCatalog;
                    if (source && typeof source.fetchThemes === 'function') {
                        return source.fetchThemes(type, subTheme);
                    }
                    if (!root.axios || typeof root.axios.get !== 'function') {
                        return Promise.resolve([]);
                    }
                    return root.axios.get('/api/guest/mitra/fetch-themes', {
                        params: {
                            type: type,
                            sub_theme: subTheme
                        }
                    }).then(function (response) {
                        var payload = response && response.data && response.data.data;
                        var themes = payload && payload.themes;
                        if (Object.prototype.toString.call(themes) === '[object Array]') return themes;
                        return asList(payload);
                    });
                }
            }
        }
    };
})(window);
</script>

    <script>
        AOS.init({
            disable: 'mobile',
            duration: 850,
            easing: 'ease-out-cubic',
            once: true,
            offset: 80
        });

        const GLOBAL_MAP_PACKAGE = [
            { label: 'Undangan Pernikahan', type: 'wedding', isOpen: 1 },
            { label: 'Undangan Khitanan', type: 'khitanan', isOpen: (window.__MITRA_BOOTSTRAP__.product.flags.khitanan ? 1 : 0) },
            { label: 'Undangan Aqiqah', type: 'aqiqah', isOpen: (window.__MITRA_BOOTSTRAP__.product.flags.aqiqah ? 1 : 0) },
            { label: 'Undangan Wisuda', type: 'wisuda', isOpen: (window.__MITRA_BOOTSTRAP__.product.flags.wisuda ? 1 : 0) },
            { label: 'Undangan Birthday', type: 'birthday', isOpen: (window.__MITRA_BOOTSTRAP__.product.flags.birthday ? 1 : 0) },
            { label: 'Undangan Bukber', type: 'bukber', isOpen: (window.__MITRA_BOOTSTRAP__.product.flags.bukber ? 1 : 0) },
            { label: 'Undangan Idul Fitri', type: 'idul_fitri', isOpen: (window.__MITRA_BOOTSTRAP__.product.flags.idul_fitri ? 1 : 0) },
            { label: 'Undangan Natal', type: 'natal', isOpen: (window.__MITRA_BOOTSTRAP__.product.flags.natal ? 1 : 0) },
            { label: 'Save The Date', type: 'save_the_date', isOpen: (window.__MITRA_BOOTSTRAP__.product.flags.save_the_date ? 1 : 0) },
        ];

        var vue = new Vue({
            el: '#katalog-tema',
            mixins: [AjakanThemePalette.mixin],
            data: {
                page: 1,
                perPage: 8,
                selectedPackage: '',
                selectedSub: '',
                listThemes: [],
                allThemes: [],
                listSubThemes: [],
                popularIds: [],
                listOtherProducts: [],
                product_detail: '',
            },
            mounted() {
                if (this.listActivePackage && this.listActivePackage.length > 0) {
                    this.changePackage(this.listActivePackage[0].type);
                }
            },
            computed: {
                displayedThemes() {
                    const startIndex = this.perPage * (this.page - 1);
                    const endIndex = startIndex + this.perPage;
                    return this.listThemes.slice(startIndex, endIndex);
                },
                listActivePackage() {
                    return GLOBAL_MAP_PACKAGE.filter((p) => !!p.isOpen);
                },
                pagerTotal() {
                    return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(this.listThemes.length / this.perPage));
                },
                pagerPages() {
                    const total = this.pagerTotal;
                    const chunk = window.innerWidth < 768 ? 3 : 5;
                    const start = Math.floor((this.page - 1) / chunk) * chunk + 1;
                    const end = Math.min(start + chunk - 1, total);
                    const pages = [];
                    for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) pages.push(i);
                    return pages;
                },
            },
            methods: {
                orderTheme() {
                    window.location.href = '/customer/redirect-login/order-bundle';
                },
                sleep(ms) {
                    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
                },
                getCategoryAttrs(subthemesId) {
                    const category = this.listSubThemes.filter((t) => t.id == subthemesId);
                    return category.length ? { category: category[0].category, name: category[0].name } : null;
                },
                getGlobalMapPackageAttrs(type) {
                    const pkg = GLOBAL_MAP_PACKAGE.filter((p) => p.type == type);
                    return pkg.length ? pkg[0] : null;
                },
                resetPage() {
                    this.page = 1;
                },
                goPagerPage(p) {
                    const next = Math.min(Math.max(1, p), this.pagerTotal);
                    if (next !== this.page) this.page = next;
                },
                filterThemesBySub() {
                    const popularId = 0;
                    if (this.selectedSub == popularId) {
                        return this.allThemes.filter((theme) => this.popularIds.some((id) => id == theme.id));
                    }
                    return this.allThemes.filter((theme) => theme.sub_theme == this.selectedSub);
                },
                selectSub(subId) {
                    this.selectedSub = subId;
                    this.resetPage();
                    this.listThemes = this.filterThemesBySub();
                },
                fetchThemes(packageType = '') {
                    let urlFetch = packageType ? '/api/guest/mitra/catalog?type=' + packageType : '/api/guest/mitra/catalog';
                    axios.get(urlFetch).then(response => {
                        const data = (response.data && response.data.data) ? response.data.data : {};
                        const rawThemes = Array.isArray(data.themes) ? data.themes : [];
                        const rawSubThemes = Array.isArray(data.sub_themes) ? data.sub_themes : [];
                        const rawPopularIds = Array.isArray(data.popular_ids) ? data.popular_ids : [];
                        this.listSubThemes = rawSubThemes;
                        this.popularIds = rawPopularIds;
                        function safePackage(idx) {
                            return GLOBAL_MAP_PACKAGE && GLOBAL_MAP_PACKAGE.length > idx ? GLOBAL_MAP_PACKAGE[idx] : null;
                        }
                        const saveTheDate = GLOBAL_MAP_PACKAGE.find((pkg) => pkg.type === 'save_the_date');
                        const wedding = safePackage(0);
                        if (saveTheDate && this.selectedPackage === saveTheDate.type) {
                            this.selectedSub = '';
                            this.allThemes = [];
                            this.listThemes = rawThemes.map((theme) => ({
                                ...theme,
                                name: theme.name,
                                category: saveTheDate.label,
                                subName: 'Save The Date'
                            }));
                        } else if (wedding && this.selectedPackage === wedding.type) {
                            this.allThemes = rawThemes.map((theme) => {
                                const catObj = this.getCategoryAttrs(theme.sub_theme);
                                const themeCategory = catObj?.category;
                                const globalMapAttrs = themeCategory ? this.getGlobalMapPackageAttrs(themeCategory) : null;
                                return { ...theme, category: globalMapAttrs?.label || '', subName: catObj?.name };
                            });
                            if (rawSubThemes.length) {
                                this.selectSub(rawSubThemes[0].id);
                            } else {
                                this.selectedSub = '';
                                this.listThemes = this.allThemes;
                            }
                        } else {
                            this.selectedSub = '';
                            this.allThemes = [];
                            this.listThemes = rawThemes.map((theme) => {
                                const catObj = this.getCategoryAttrs(theme.sub_theme);
                                const themeCategory = catObj?.category;
                                const globalMapAttrs = themeCategory ? this.getGlobalMapPackageAttrs(themeCategory) : null;
                                return { ...theme, category: globalMapAttrs?.label || '', subName: catObj?.name };
                            });
                        }
                    }).catch(error => {
                        console.log(error);
                    });
                },
                changePackage(packageType) {
                    this.resetPage();
                    this.selectedSub = '';
                    this.selectedPackage = packageType;
                    this.fetchThemes(packageType);
                },
            }
        });
    </script>

<script>
(function () {
    var GAP = 12;
    var nav = document.getElementById('rg-nav');
    var spacer = document.getElementById('rg-nav-spacer');
    var btn = document.getElementById('rg-mobile-menu-btn');
    var menu = document.getElementById('rg-mobile-menu');
    var icon = document.getElementById('rg-mobile-menu-icon');

    function syncOffset() {
        if (!nav) return;
        var offset = Math.ceil(nav.getBoundingClientRect().bottom) + GAP;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--rg-nav-offset', offset + 'px');
        if (spacer) spacer.style.height = offset + 'px';
    }

    function onScroll() {
        if (!nav) return;
        nav.classList.toggle('rg-nav-scrolled', window.scrollY > 12);
    }

    function normalizePath(pathname) {
        var p = (pathname || '/').replace(/\/+$/, '');
        return p === '' ? '/' : p;
    }

    function syncActiveNav() {
        var path = normalizePath(window.location.pathname);
        var hash = (window.location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
        var links = document.querySelectorAll('[data-rg-nav]');
        var activeKey = '';

        if (path.indexOf('about-us') !== -1) {
            activeKey = hash === 'pricing' ? 'pricing' : 'feature';
        } else if (path.indexOf('product') !== -1) {
            activeKey = 'product';
        } else if (path.indexOf('blog') !== -1) {
            activeKey = 'blog';
        } else if (path === '/') {
            activeKey = hash === 'katalog-tema' ? 'tema' : 'home';
        }

        links.forEach(function (link) {
            var key = link.getAttribute('data-rg-nav');
            link.classList.toggle('is-active', key === activeKey);
        });
    }

    function setMenuOpen(open) {
        if (!menu || !btn) return;
        menu.classList.toggle('hidden', !open);
        if (nav) nav.classList.toggle('rg-nav-open', open);
        btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');
        if (icon) {
            icon.classList.toggle('fa-bars', !open);
            icon.classList.toggle('fa-times', open);
        }
        document.body.style.overflow = open ? 'hidden' : '';
        syncOffset();
    }

    syncOffset();
    onScroll();
    syncActiveNav();
    window.addEventListener('load', syncOffset);
    window.addEventListener('resize', syncOffset);
    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', syncActiveNav);

    if (typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined' && nav) {
        new ResizeObserver(syncOffset).observe(nav);
    }

    if (btn && menu) {
        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
            setMenuOpen(menu.classList.contains('hidden'));
        });
        menu.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(function (link) {
            link.addEventListener('click', function () { setMenuOpen(false); });
        });
    }
})();
</script>

<script>
(function() {
    function fallbackBtn() {
        var nodes = document.querySelectorAll('#modern-wa-btn');
        for (var i = 1; i < nodes.length; i++) nodes[i].remove();
        return nodes[0] || null;
    }

    function hideIfCustomWa() {
        var btn = fallbackBtn();
        if (!btn) return false;
        // Only hide fallback when floating widget button is actually rendered
        var customBtn = document.querySelector('.floating-wpp .floating-wpp-button');
        if (customBtn && customBtn.offsetWidth > 0 && customBtn.offsetHeight > 0) {
            btn.style.display = 'none';
            return true;
        }
        btn.style.display = '';
        return false;
    }

    function bindDrag(btn) {
        if (!btn || btn.dataset.waBound) return;
        btn.dataset.waBound = '1';
        let offset = { x: 0, y: 0 };
        let isDown = false;

        btn.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {
        isDown = true;
        offset = {
            x: btn.offsetLeft - e.clientX,
            y: btn.offsetTop - e.clientY
        };
        btn.style.transition = "none";
        document.body.style.userSelect = "none";
    }, false);

    document.addEventListener('mouseup', function() {
        isDown = false;
        btn.style.transition = "";
        document.body.style.userSelect = "";
    }, false);

    document.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e) {
        if (!isDown) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        let newLeft = e.clientX + offset.x;
        let newTop = e.clientY + offset.y;
        newLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerWidth - btn.offsetWidth, newLeft));
        newTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight - btn.offsetHeight, newTop));
        btn.style.left = newLeft + "px";
        btn.style.top = newTop + "px";
        btn.style.right = "auto";
        btn.style.bottom = "auto";
    }, false);

    btn.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) {
        isDown = true;
        const touch = e.touches[0];
        offset = {
            x: btn.offsetLeft - touch.clientX,
            y: btn.offsetTop - touch.clientY
        };
        btn.style.transition = "none";
    }, false);

    btn.addEventListener('touchend', function() {
        isDown = false;
        btn.style.transition = "";
    }, false);

    btn.addEventListener('touchmove', function(e) {
        if (!isDown) return;
        const touch = e.touches[0];
        let newLeft = touch.clientX + offset.x;
        let newTop = touch.clientY + offset.y;
        newLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerWidth - btn.offsetWidth, newLeft));
        newTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight - btn.offsetHeight, newTop));
        btn.style.left = newLeft + "px";
        btn.style.top = newTop + "px";
        btn.style.right = "auto";
        btn.style.bottom = "auto";
    }, { passive: true });

        function setInitialPosition() {
            btn.style.left = "";
            btn.style.top = "";
            btn.style.right = "1rem";
            btn.style.bottom = "1.25rem";
        }
        setInitialPosition();
        window.addEventListener('resize', setInitialPosition);
    }

    function sync() {
        var btn = fallbackBtn();
        if (btn) bindDrag(btn);
        hideIfCustomWa();
    }

    document.addEventListener('mitra:ready', sync);
    sync();
    setTimeout(sync, 700);
})();
</script>
<script>
(function () {
    var reduceMotion = window.matchMedia ? window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)') : null;
    var selector = [
        '[class*=\"animate-\"]',
        '.blob',
        '.fade-in',
        '.still-track',
        '.cover-slide-img',
        '.pc-hero__glow-orb'
    ].join(',');
    var nodes = [];
    var observer = null;

    function setPaused(el, paused) {
        if (!el || !el.style) return;
        el.style.animationPlayState = paused ? 'paused' : 'running';
    }

    function pauseByVisibility() {
        var hidden = document.hidden || (reduceMotion && reduceMotion.matches);
        for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
            if (hidden) {
                setPaused(nodes[i], true);
            } else if (!observer) {
                setPaused(nodes[i], false);
            } else {
                setPaused(nodes[i], nodes[i].__ajOffscreen === true);
            }
        }
    }

    function bindObserver() {
        if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) return;
        observer = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
            entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                entry.target.__ajOffscreen = !entry.isIntersecting;
                setPaused(entry.target, document.hidden || entry.target.__ajOffscreen || (reduceMotion && reduceMotion.matches));
            });
        }, { threshold: 0.05 });
        for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) observer.observe(nodes[i]);
    }

    function refreshMotionNodes() {
        if (observer) observer.disconnect();
        observer = null;
        nodes = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(selector));
        if (!nodes.length) return;
        if (!(reduceMotion && reduceMotion.matches)) bindObserver();
        pauseByVisibility();
    }

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', pauseByVisibility);
    document.addEventListener('mitra:ready', refreshMotionNodes);
    if (reduceMotion && typeof reduceMotion.addEventListener === 'function') {
        reduceMotion.addEventListener('change', refreshMotionNodes);
    }
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', refreshMotionNodes, { once: true });
    } else {
        refreshMotionNodes();
    }
})();
</script>

        function mountAboutPricingTabs() {
            var root = document.getElementById('pricing');
            if (!root || typeof window.Vue !== 'function' || root.__pricingVue__) return;

            var initialTab = 'website';
            if (!root.querySelector('#pills-website-tab')) {
                if (root.querySelector('#pills-savethedate-tab')) initialTab = 'savethedate';
                else if (root.querySelector('#pills-otherEvents-tab')) initialTab = 'otherEvents';
            }

            root.__pricingVue__ = new Vue({
                el: '#pricing',
                data: {
                    activePricingTab: initialTab
                }
            });
        }

        // document ready jquery
        $(document).ready(function() {
            AOS.init({
                disable: 'mobile',
                duration: 850,
                easing: 'ease-out-cubic',
                once: true,
                offset: 80
            });
            mountAboutPricingTabs();
        });
    </script>

<script>
(function () {
    var GAP = 12;
    var nav = document.getElementById('rg-nav');
    var spacer = document.getElementById('rg-nav-spacer');
    var btn = document.getElementById('rg-mobile-menu-btn');
    var menu = document.getElementById('rg-mobile-menu');
    var icon = document.getElementById('rg-mobile-menu-icon');

    function syncOffset() {
        if (!nav) return;
        var offset = Math.ceil(nav.getBoundingClientRect().bottom) + GAP;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--rg-nav-offset', offset + 'px');
        if (spacer) spacer.style.height = offset + 'px';
    }

    function onScroll() {
        if (!nav) return;
        nav.classList.toggle('rg-nav-scrolled', window.scrollY > 12);
    }

    function normalizePath(pathname) {
        var p = (pathname || '/').replace(/\/+$/, '');
        return p === '' ? '/' : p;
    }

    function syncActiveNav() {
        var path = normalizePath(window.location.pathname);
        var hash = (window.location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
        var links = document.querySelectorAll('[data-rg-nav]');
        var activeKey = '';

        if (path.indexOf('about-us') !== -1) {
            activeKey = hash === 'pricing' ? 'pricing' : 'feature';
        } else if (path.indexOf('product') !== -1) {
            activeKey = 'product';
        } else if (path.indexOf('blog') !== -1) {
            activeKey = 'blog';
        } else if (path === '/') {
            activeKey = hash === 'katalog-tema' ? 'tema' : 'home';
        }

        links.forEach(function (link) {
            var key = link.getAttribute('data-rg-nav');
            link.classList.toggle('is-active', key === activeKey);
        });
    }

    function setMenuOpen(open) {
        if (!menu || !btn) return;
        menu.classList.toggle('hidden', !open);
        if (nav) nav.classList.toggle('rg-nav-open', open);
        btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');
        if (icon) {
            icon.classList.toggle('fa-bars', !open);
            icon.classList.toggle('fa-times', open);
        }
        document.body.style.overflow = open ? 'hidden' : '';
        syncOffset();
    }

    syncOffset();
    onScroll();
    syncActiveNav();
    window.addEventListener('load', syncOffset);
    window.addEventListener('resize', syncOffset);
    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', syncActiveNav);

    if (typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined' && nav) {
        new ResizeObserver(syncOffset).observe(nav);
    }

    if (btn && menu) {
        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
            setMenuOpen(menu.classList.contains('hidden'));
        });
        menu.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(function (link) {
            link.addEventListener('click', function () { setMenuOpen(false); });
        });
    }
})();
</script>

<script>
(function() {
    function fallbackBtn() {
        var nodes = document.querySelectorAll('#modern-wa-btn');
        for (var i = 1; i < nodes.length; i++) nodes[i].remove();
        return nodes[0] || null;
    }

    function hideIfCustomWa() {
        var btn = fallbackBtn();
        if (!btn) return false;
        // Only hide fallback when floating widget button is actually rendered
        var customBtn = document.querySelector('.floating-wpp .floating-wpp-button');
        if (customBtn && customBtn.offsetWidth > 0 && customBtn.offsetHeight > 0) {
            btn.style.display = 'none';
            return true;
        }
        btn.style.display = '';
        return false;
    }

    function bindDrag(btn) {
        if (!btn || btn.dataset.waBound) return;
        btn.dataset.waBound = '1';
        let offset = { x: 0, y: 0 };
        let isDown = false;

        btn.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {
        isDown = true;
        offset = {
            x: btn.offsetLeft - e.clientX,
            y: btn.offsetTop - e.clientY
        };
        btn.style.transition = "none";
        document.body.style.userSelect = "none";
    }, false);

    document.addEventListener('mouseup', function() {
        isDown = false;
        btn.style.transition = "";
        document.body.style.userSelect = "";
    }, false);

    document.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e) {
        if (!isDown) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        let newLeft = e.clientX + offset.x;
        let newTop = e.clientY + offset.y;
        newLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerWidth - btn.offsetWidth, newLeft));
        newTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight - btn.offsetHeight, newTop));
        btn.style.left = newLeft + "px";
        btn.style.top = newTop + "px";
        btn.style.right = "auto";
        btn.style.bottom = "auto";
    }, false);

    btn.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) {
        isDown = true;
        const touch = e.touches[0];
        offset = {
            x: btn.offsetLeft - touch.clientX,
            y: btn.offsetTop - touch.clientY
        };
        btn.style.transition = "none";
    }, false);

    btn.addEventListener('touchend', function() {
        isDown = false;
        btn.style.transition = "";
    }, false);

    btn.addEventListener('touchmove', function(e) {
        if (!isDown) return;
        const touch = e.touches[0];
        let newLeft = touch.clientX + offset.x;
        let newTop = touch.clientY + offset.y;
        newLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerWidth - btn.offsetWidth, newLeft));
        newTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight - btn.offsetHeight, newTop));
        btn.style.left = newLeft + "px";
        btn.style.top = newTop + "px";
        btn.style.right = "auto";
        btn.style.bottom = "auto";
    }, { passive: true });

        function setInitialPosition() {
            btn.style.left = "";
            btn.style.top = "";
            btn.style.right = "1rem";
            btn.style.bottom = "1.25rem";
        }
        setInitialPosition();
        window.addEventListener('resize', setInitialPosition);
    }

    function sync() {
        var btn = fallbackBtn();
        if (btn) bindDrag(btn);
        hideIfCustomWa();
    }

    document.addEventListener('mitra:ready', sync);
    sync();
    setTimeout(sync, 700);
})();
</script>
<script>
(function () {
    var reduceMotion = window.matchMedia ? window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)') : null;
    var selector = [
        '[class*=\"animate-\"]',
        '.blob',
        '.fade-in',
        '.still-track',
        '.cover-slide-img',
        '.pc-hero__glow-orb'
    ].join(',');
    var nodes = [];
    var observer = null;

    function setPaused(el, paused) {
        if (!el || !el.style) return;
        el.style.animationPlayState = paused ? 'paused' : 'running';
    }

    function pauseByVisibility() {
        var hidden = document.hidden || (reduceMotion && reduceMotion.matches);
        for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
            if (hidden) {
                setPaused(nodes[i], true);
            } else if (!observer) {
                setPaused(nodes[i], false);
            } else {
                setPaused(nodes[i], nodes[i].__ajOffscreen === true);
            }
        }
    }

    function bindObserver() {
        if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) return;
        observer = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
            entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                entry.target.__ajOffscreen = !entry.isIntersecting;
                setPaused(entry.target, document.hidden || entry.target.__ajOffscreen || (reduceMotion && reduceMotion.matches));
            });
        }, { threshold: 0.05 });
        for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) observer.observe(nodes[i]);
    }

    function refreshMotionNodes() {
        if (observer) observer.disconnect();
        observer = null;
        nodes = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(selector));
        if (!nodes.length) return;
        if (!(reduceMotion && reduceMotion.matches)) bindObserver();
        pauseByVisibility();
    }

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', pauseByVisibility);
    document.addEventListener('mitra:ready', refreshMotionNodes);
    if (reduceMotion && typeof reduceMotion.addEventListener === 'function') {
        reduceMotion.addEventListener('change', refreshMotionNodes);
    }
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', refreshMotionNodes, { once: true });
    } else {
        refreshMotionNodes();
    }
})();
</script>
